# Truth sweep — W477 (2026-09-19)

**Why this document exists.** Two measured MILESTONE M1 audits found 14 (ledger v4) and then 27 (ledger v5) Tier-1
truth defects with almost no overlap: each audit is capped at ten findings per region, so it samples. On the Owner's
ruling of 2026-09-19 ("a wider, one-time sweep for this kind of problem to get there faster") W477 swept EVERY reached
frontend surface against the ten defect classes the audits had found, with no cap, and had every finding attacked by
a skeptic who reproduced it before it stood.

**Coverage.** 13 shards built deterministically before any agent ran (`inventory.py`): 83 of the 114 frontend files —
every page, every component and helper that calls the API or renders a figure, and the shell's `packages/ui` — and the
273 distinct `/api` paths they call, followed into their handlers and every other writer of the same claim. The 31
files left out call no API and render no figure. A backend booted from HEAD `929508f0` (plus W476's docs) served the
reproductions at :8087 on the native floor (`AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1`). Each shard's own coverage note is in §4.

**Result.** 228 findings reported; **190 reproduced by the skeptics** (38 rejected — §3).
Tier-1 (a truth defect on a reached surface): **106** · Tier-2 (an invisible shortfall): **84**. 56 of the reproduced findings overlap
an entry of ledger v5 (`known_as`) — most as ANOTHER writer of the same untruth that the ledger did not name.

| class | Tier-1 | Tier-2 |
|---|---|---|
| C1 certifies what it could not assess | 13 | 10 |
| C2 keyword screen presented as a judgement | 4 | 4 |
| C3 invented or constant readings | 16 | 9 |
| C4 present-tense claims about processes not running | 12 | 11 |
| C5 a second writer or the reached page disagrees with the API | 19 | 16 |
| C6 faith-content fidelity | 4 | 2 |
| C7 missing provenance | 12 | 16 |
| C8 figures that cannot fail | 11 | 1 |
| C9 a decision that breaks its own rule | 3 | 2 |
| C10 counts or lists that do not match what is served | 12 | 13 |

Every reproduced finding is a register row's evidence: the rows are one per emitting file and tier (`sweep <file>`),
and cite the IDs below. IDs are `S<shard>.<index>`.

## 1. Tier-1 — truth defects on reached surfaces, by emitting file

- **S5.4 · C7 · /authorship Authorship Intelligence (APIE): all 9 stage cards and the 'Authorship Pipeline Complete' banner**
  - shown: Every stage (Source Discovery, Peer Review, Integrity Audit, Publication Readiness...) gets a green CheckCircle and 'Stage n of 9', then 'Authorship Pipeline Complete'. The header says 'Powered by Nine Cognitive Engines + MJM'. No provenance chip appears anywhere.

The label 'Topic/Thesis:' is also not matched by the floor's _field('Topic') regex, so the user's topic is dropped. A peer review, integrity audit or publication-readiness check is ticked green when none was performed. Across the 9 stage contents 'tree canopy' occurs 0 times (its only occurrence is the init event), while 'beekeepers'/'beekeepers yorkshire' occur about 52 times. Stage 1: '## Primary Source Categories ... - small-scale beekeepers - landscape small-scale - beekeepers yorkshire'. The beekeeper text is another request's data (the bees_notes ingest / Genesis beekeeper journey in DATA_DIR). No event carries served_by.

Show a neutral (not green) mark for floor stages and a red mark for '[... pending' stages. Label the floor's 'Peer Review' and 'Integrity Audit' as 'scaffold — no review performed'. Make the floor's _field accept 'Topic/Thesis'.
  - skeptic: Reproduced in part. POST /intelligence/authorship with the tree-canopy topic returns 9 floor-served stages. 'tree canopy' appears 0 times in the stage content, so the topic is dropped and the floor restates the persona prompt ('grounded in: You are a Senior Research Librarian...'). No event carries served_by, and AuthorshipEngine.tsx has no floor/provenance label. The emerald CheckCircle at :364 is unconditional, and the 'Authorship Pipeline Complete' banner renders. The beekeeper…

So the verdict is a constant set by the threshold (none accepted at 0.85, all at 0.8 or below), whatever the claims say.

(1) Proposals are not deduplicated (clicking Propose Advance twice creates two), so approving a stale concept->prototype proposal after the project has reached commercialise moves the stage BACK to prototype, while the page says 'stage advanced'. ## 2. Tier-2 — invisible shortfalls, by emitting file

### `agentic_core/api/change_control.py` — 5

- **S1.10 · C1 · Change Control Agency page > 'Implement' button on an approved change, and the 'Implemented' stat and filter**
  - shown: After Implement, the record shows status 'IMPLEMENTED' (blue Zap) and counts toward the 'Implemented' stat.
  - truth: A change submitted from this page's own form never carries config_change, so /implement applies nothing (applied: null) and still marks it implemented. The W318 rule (never claim 'implemented' when no live behaviour changed) covers only a config with no consumer, not a change with no config at all.
  - evidence: POST /api/v1/cca/submit {title:'w477 probe: raise gateway temperature', description:'Raise the gateway temperature to 0.9…', change_type:'config_minor', submitted_by:'workstation-ui'} → cca-84dfb95a55 auto-approved. POST /api/v1/cca/cca-84dfb95a55/implement → {status:'implemented', applied:null}. GET shows config_change None and last audit event 'implemented'. Code: change_control.py:1191-1236.
  - writers: agentic_core/api/change_control.py:1227-1236 (marks implemented regardless of spec); apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/ChangeControlAgency.tsx:284-293 (Implement offered for any approved non-economy change), :322-324 (form sends no config_change), :449/:505/:527 (Implemented count)
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): When config_change is empty, record 'acknowledged — nothing to apply' (a distinct status) or refuse, and don't count it as Implemented. Either let the form carry a config_change or say that it cannot.
  - skeptic: Reproduced. POST /cca/submit from the UI shape produced cca-3262f21da4 (auto-approved). POST /implement then returned status 'implemented' with applied:null. The UI form cannot attach config_change, so Implement never applies anything and the page gives no hint of that. Downgraded to tier 2: 'Implement' is an operator-attested status for changes that may be applied outside the system, so it is a silent shortfall rather than a fabricated certification.

- **S1.11 · C1 · Change Control Agency page > submit result + 'AUTO' badge + 'decided by' line + tier legend note** *(overlaps ledger v5 same composite as v5 R3.5 (review rule) and v5 R6.0; the LOW auto-approval…)*
  - shown: '✓ Change auto-approved (LOW tier + healthy organism)'. 'decided by: LOW-tier auto-approval (organism healthy, no immune threat)'. 'LOW tier changes are auto-approved when organism health ≥ 60%…'. The review_result reads 'Auto-approved: LOW impact, organism healthy'.
  - truth: 'Healthy' is composite_health: 40% a defaulted 1.0 (no self-healing circuits tracked) and 20% simulated ATP, which only rises (v5 R6.1). With ATP at 1.0, 0.4·immune + 0.4 + 0.2 ≥ 0.6 for any immune value, so the health condition cannot fail. The transformation orchestrator also files every run as config_minor (LOW) and is auto-approved this way, which is what its stage 7 'Change Control (arms-length) ✓' rests on.
  - evidence: GET /api/v1/organism/status → composite_health 0.929; terms self_healing {value 1.0, measured false, 'defaulted to 1.0, not a measurement'}, metabolic {measured false, 'simulated'}. Transformation CCA cca-7604ac8b66: change_type config_minor, decision_source low_tier_auto_approval, review_result 'Auto-approved: LOW impact, organism healthy, immune threat NOMINAL.'
  - writers: agentic_core/api/change_control.py:558-564 (rule + 'organism healthy' sentence), :14-15 (docstring); agentic_core/api/transformation_orchestration.py:237-244 (self-files config_minor); apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/ChangeControlAgency.tsx:63, :326-328, :395
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Gate on composite_health_measured_only, or say 'composite (60% defaulted/simulated)' in the record and the UI. Have the orchestrator file under a type whose tier matches what it does.
  - skeptic: Code and live confirmed. change_control.py:558 requires composite_health >= 0.6. GET /organism/status gives composite 0.96, of which 40% is self_healing 'defaulted to 1.0, not a measurement' and 20% is simulated metabolic 1.0, so the health term cannot fail (0.4*immune + 0.6 >= 0.6). Only the immune-threat clause can hold a change. Downgraded to tier 2: measured-only health is 0.9, so 'organism healthy' is not untrue today. The defect is a gate that cannot fail, and the page does not say so.

- **S1.12 · C7 · Change Control Agency page > expanded card > 'Review — approved' block (review_result)**
  - shown: 'DECIDED BY RULE, NOT BY THE MODEL … The prose below is the model's and had NO bearing on the decision. --- model output --- …'
  - truth: The prose is the deterministic floor, not a model. gateway.query's text is used with no served_by, so the record labels floor output as 'model output' / 'the model's'. On CRITICAL holds the text also reads 'The serving model recommended…'.
  - evidence: POST /api/v1/cca/submit {change_type:'organism_mutation'} → cca-ada266d4f7. POST …/review {reviewer_notes:'Requested via the Change Control Agency UI'} → review_result '…The prose below is the model's… --- model output --- _[Workstation native structured engine — owned, no external dependency]_ … Structured go-to-market frame for: You are the Chief Governance Officer…'.
  - writers: agentic_core/api/change_control.py:927 (text only, no provenance), :944 ('--- model output ---'), :958-963, :978-981 ('the model's'); apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/ChangeControlAgency.tsx:195-201 (renders it)
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Capture served_by from the gateway call. When it is the floor, label the block 'structured floor output — no model reviewed this change' instead of 'model output'.
  - skeptic: Reproduced. POST /cca/{id}/review on an organism_mutation change returned review_result 'DECIDED BY RULE, NOT BY THE MODEL … The prose below is the model's … --- model output --- _[Workstation native structured engine…]_'. The floor output is labelled 'the model's'. Tier 2 rather than 1: the record says plainly that the prose had no bearing on the decision, and the engine banner sits directly under the label.

- **S1.13 · C1 · Change Control Agency page > expanded HIGH card > '§17.5 pre-validation: PASS (no twin model — health gate only)'** *(overlaps ledger v5 v5 R6.0)*
  - shown: A green 'PASS' pre-validation verdict on the approved HIGH change.
  - truth: The label names a health gate, but the gate is the same composite (60% defaulted/simulated) that cannot fail. The green PASS is what /implement accepts as the §17.5 invariant.
  - evidence: cca-ada266d4f7 twin_prevalidation {verdict:'pass', source:'health_gate_default', composite_health_at_sim:0.96, source_label:'no twin model — health gate only', method '…the proposed change was NOT simulated'}.
  - writers: agentic_core/api/change_control.py:771-797; apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/ChangeControlAgency.tsx:204-215
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Show the verdict as 'not assessable — no twin model' rather than a green PASS, or gate on measured-only health (as v5 R6.0 proposes).
  - skeptic: Reproduced. twin_prevalidation {verdict:'pass', source:'health_gate_default', source_label:'no twin model — health gate only'} is shown as a green PASS. The label honestly says it is a health gate, not a simulation. The remaining shortfall is that the health gate cannot fail (the same root as [11]), and the page does not disclose that. Tier 2, not 1.

- **S1.18 · C2 · Change Control Agency page > Submit form tier indicator vs recorded tier**
  - shown: Before submitting, the form shows 'Tier: LOW' for 'Config — Minor'. After submitting, a green '✓ Change submitted — awaiting review'.
  - truth: A substring screen on the description changes the tier: 'constitution' or 'delete all' makes it CRITICAL. A typo fix on 'the constitution page footer' became CRITICAL, and the page never says why.
  - evidence: POST /api/v1/cca/submit {change_type:'config_minor', description:'Fix a typo in the constitution page footer'} → impact_tier CRITICAL (cca-9333b75727); description '…delete all unused feature flags from the docs page' → CRITICAL (cca-5d0bd1e680).
  - writers: agentic_core/api/change_control.py:294-300 (_determine_tier keyword list); apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/ChangeControlAgency.tsx:390-395, :326-328
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Return the elevation reason (the matched phrase) and show it. Or show the returned tier in the success message.
  - skeptic: Reproduced. A config_minor submit with the description 'Fix a typo in the constitution page footer' returned impact_tier CRITICAL, while the form had shown 'Tier: LOW'. The cause is the keyword list at change_control.py:296, and the page never says why the tier changed. Tier 2 stands.

VSBCockpit.tsx:265 hard-codes en-GB, so dictation there does not follow the language setting.

Browsers without Web Speech (Firefox) get no dictation at all, and per-language support is never checked. This is a partial shortfall the surface does not disclose.

A grep shows useAdaptiveUI is consumed only as { layout, emotionalAdjustment } in the six hubs, where it renders the two badges (e.g. EmploymentHub.tsx:45-46). No other affordance or AI request reads guidedMode or tone. The badges do reflect the setting, so the overstatement is low-severity (tier 2).

  - truth: The list is `git log` of the server's working directory (the Workstation codebase itself), not the user's project activity. ### `agentic_core/api/intelligence.py` — 1

- **S6.4 · C7 · /intelligence BDP/SPI output (also /ceo chat and /transformation AI Assess): generation contaminated by other requests' recalled memory**
  - shown: Stage analyses for the user's challenge, 'Positioning wedge from small-scale beekeepers', 'Positioning wedge from beekeepers yorkshire', ...
  - truth: The gateway's default augment=True puts token-overlap recall of OTHER interactions (another run's Yorkshire beekeeper Genesis journey, stored in memory.json) into the prompt. The floor then builds its 'salient terms' from that recall instead of the user's challenge. For the challenge 'Open a halal bakery in Leeds selling to cafes', the word 'bakery' appears only in the 'Processing:' echo, while 'beekeepers' appears 25 times across the stages. The output is then written back to memory (gateway.py:179), which makes the effect grow. The CEO chat shows the same thing: its 'salient terms' ('service commercialisation', 'redistribution service') come from memory.json, not from the question.
  - evidence: grep on C:/tmp/w477_s6/bdp.txt: 'bakery' x1 (init echo), 'beekeepers' x25, 'beekeepers yorkshire' x21. C:/tmp/w477b/memory.json contains both 'beekeepers' and 'redistribution service'. CEO chat response (C:/tmp/w477_s6/ceo.txt) salient terms: service commercialisation / redistribution service / owner digital-twin.
  - writers: agentic_core/api/intelligence.py:415 (gateway.query with default augment=True), :315, :344, :530, :636, :698, :718, :739, :761; agentic_core/ai/gateway.py:92-111 (_augment), :117/:132 (augment default True), :148, :179 (memory write-back); agentic_core/api/v138/ceo.py:~432 (stream_meta augment=True); agentic_core/api/transformation.py:207; apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/Settings.tsx:176-177 ('Only what you type here is used — nothing is inferred from your activity'), which is contradicted…
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Pass augment=False on the intelligence/CEO/assess generation paths (the W332 rule for content that is shown or saved). Or keep recall out of what the floor treats as the subject, and label recall-shaped output as such.
  - skeptic: Mechanism confirmed, but the contamination was not reproduced fresh, so I downgraded to tier 2 (intermittent, depends on memory state). Code: intelligence.py:415 calls gateway.query with the default augment=True. ceo.py:432 passes augment=True explicitly. That goes against the gateway's own W332 rule that generation callers pass augment=False. gateway.py:92-111 injects token-overlap recall of other interactions, and :179 writes every output back to memory. The sweeper's artifact from this…

### `agentic_core/optimizer/engine.py` — 1

- **S4.21 · C5 · Resource Fabric (/fabric) - Run on 'Resource Allocation'**
  - shown: Allocation result: domain 'general', share {cpu:0.1, priority:0}, status 'ACTIVE', and the top-level status 'SUCCESS' for tier 'standard' and domain 'science'
  - truth: The requested domain is dropped: RALVerifier's result has no domain, so the allocator falls back to 'general'. 'standard' is not a quota tier, so the allocator gives the free-tier share while the scheduler treats it as paid (immediate execution). The ACTIVE pool belongs to a per-request optimiser instance and is discarded when the response returns. The simulated-capacity note is shown.
  - evidence: POST /api/v1/optimizer/allocate {domain:'science', requirements:{CPU:4,RAM:1024}, tier:'standard'} -> allocation {domain:'general', share:{cpu:0.1, priority:0}, status:'ACTIVE'}. Code: optimizer/engine.py:47-48, allocator.py:15-21, scheduler.py:27-43.

  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Pass req.domain through. Reject unknown tiers or map them explicitly. Label the allocation 'computed, not held'.
  - skeptic: Reproduced. POST /api/v1/optimizer/allocate {domain:'science', tier:'standard'} returned status SUCCESS and allocation {domain:'general', share:{cpu:0.1, priority:0}, status:'ACTIVE'}. The requested domain is silently replaced and the free-tier share is applied. The simulated-capacity note is present, but it does not disclose the domain drop or the tier mismatch. Tier 2.

'(sim)' implies a simulation over this run; it is a constant plus a cross-run running total.

### `apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/BusinessPlan.tsx` — 1

- **S3.15 · C5 · Business Plan 'Chief: deliver via tree' button (BusinessPlan.tsx:87-96)**
  - shown: The spinner stops and nothing appears. The page's own comment (W329) says 'actions never fail silently'.
  - truth: A 409 from a pending Mode-3 review gate, or any non-ok or non-JSON answer, is swallowed. The Owner is not told the delivery was refused.
  - evidence: With a design gate pending on vsb-70129c79eb, POST /api/v1/business-plan/objective/obj-80a8cfd1/orchestrate returned 409 'review gate blocks progress … orchestrate refused'. The handler only sets a result when r.ok && data.tree, and the catch is empty.
  - writers: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/BusinessPlan.tsx:87-96
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): setActErr with the response's detail.error on !r.ok and in the catch.
  - skeptic: Reproduced. I set a design review gate on vsb-69600824d0, and POST /business-plan/objective/obj-b6c539a4/orchestrate returned HTTP 409 'orchestrate refused'. I removed the gate afterwards. BusinessPlan.tsx:87-96 only sets a result when r.ok && data.tree, never calls setActErr, and has an empty catch. The Owner sees the spinner stop and nothing else.

### `apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/evolution/SovereignEvolution.tsx` — 1

- **S13.6 · C5 · /sovereign-evolution - on page load (Organism Introspection snapshot)**
  - shown: Page load shows 'Organism Introspection' (Projects, Immune Health, CPU, Items Proceeding) and directives as the current state
  - truth: GET /roadmap returns the LAST saved cycle, which can be arbitrarily old. The page shows cycle_id and duration but not created_at, so a stale snapshot (CPU %, immune health from whenever the cycle ran) reads as current. The Immune Health tile is also coloured amber for 'NOMINAL', because the tone tests threat_level against 'NONE'/'LOW', values immune.status() never emits. The tile is therefore amber for every real reading.
  - evidence: agentic_core/api/sovereign_evolution.py:314-319 (returns saved roadmap with created_at). SovereignEvolution.tsx:49-54, :146 (no created_at), :150 (tone NONE/LOW). agentic_core/organism/immune.py:70-77 (levels NOMINAL/ELEVATED/HIGH/CRITICAL only). Live introspection.immune.threat_level 'NOMINAL'.
  - writers: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/evolution/SovereignEvolution.tsx:146, :150
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Show 'cycle run <created_at>' and label the snapshot 'as of that cycle'. Tone the tile from NOMINAL/ELEVATED/HIGH/CRITICAL.
  - skeptic: Reproduced. On page load, live GET /roadmap returns the saved cycle with created_at 2026-09-19T13:53:01Z and cpu_percent 47.7. SovereignEvolution.tsx:146 shows only cycle_id and duration_ms, never created_at, so the old snapshot reads as current. The Immune Health tone at :150 tests threat_level against 'NONE'/'LOW', but the live value is 'NOMINAL' (immune.py emits NOMINAL/ELEVATED/HIGH/CRITICAL), so the tile is amber ('warn') for a nominal reading.

### `apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/organism/HeartbeatMonitor.tsx` — 1

- **S9.5 · C4 · Organism hub > Heartbeat tab: the Autonomy toggles 'Self-run' and 'Self-defend'**
  - shown: 'Self-run: Operates each living VSB on the beat…' and 'Self-defend: Re-screens every living VSB against §11 compliance each beat, so a verdict is not frozen at establishment.'
  - truth: Each beat operates ONE VSB (operate_one, least-recently-operated) and re-screens ONE VSB (_compliance_beat, round-robin). With N living VSBs, each is tended about once every N beats (N minutes at the 60s cadence). The §11 screen it re-runs is the keyword screen of R1.0/R3.4.
  - evidence: heartbeat.py:242-250 (operate_one()), :286-292 → :481-491 (_compliance_beat picks sorted(...)[0]); living_vsbs.py:194-212 ('Autonomously operate the least-recently-operated living VSB: one virtual economy cycle').
  - writers: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/organism/HeartbeatMonitor.tsx:26-29
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): 'Operates one living VSB per beat, round-robin (each is visited every N beats)' and 'Re-screens one living VSB per beat with the §11 keyword screen, round-robin'.
  - skeptic: Code confirmed. HeartbeatMonitor.tsx:26-29 says Self-run 'Operates each living VSB on the beat' and Self-defend 'Re-screens every living VSB … each beat'. heartbeat.py:242 calls operate_one() (least-recently-operated, one VSB), and _compliance_beat (:481-491) re-screens sorted(...)[0], one VSB per beat. The sibling Self-ship copy states 'ONE stale repo per beat', so these two overstate the cadence without saying so.

### `apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/organism/OrganismAnatomy.tsx` — 1

- **S8.13 · C5 · /organism?tab=anatomy — Composite health card**
  - shown: Blended figure captioned 'blended (20% simulated)'; the self_healing_health term chip reads 'SIMULATED'
  - truth: While no circuits are tracked, 60% of the blend is unmeasured: 40% defaulted self-healing plus 20% simulated ATP. The card's own health_basis line reads '40% measured'. Self-healing is a default, not a simulation. The caption is a hard-coded constant.
  - evidence: GET /api/v1/organism/health-summary → health_basis '40% measured; not measured: self_healing_health (…defaulted to 1.0…); metabolic (…simulated…)'. OrganismAnatomy.tsx:233 literal '20% simulated'; :241 prints 'SIMULATED' for any measured:false term.
  - writers: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/organism/OrganismAnatomy.tsx:233, :240-241
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Compute the caption from the terms (100 minus measured weight) and print each term's basis word ('defaulted' / 'simulated') instead of a blanket SIMULATED.
  - skeptic: Reproduced. Live /organism/health-summary shows self_healing weight 0.4 'defaulted to 1.0, not a measurement' plus metabolic 0.2 'simulated', with health_basis '40% measured'. OrganismAnatomy.tsx:233 hard-codes the caption 'blended (20% simulated)', and :241 labels the defaulted self-healing term 'SIMULATED'. The card understates the unmeasured share (60%, not 20%), and the basis on the same card contradicts it. Tier 2.

### `apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/projects/ProjectsHub.tsx` — 1

- **S12.9 · C5 · /projects - Run stream error handling**
  - shown: After a failed run the project keeps its spinning 'running' status dot, 'Run <stage>' stays disabled, and no error is shown.
  - truth: The backend emits data:{error} and sets project.status='error'. The page throws inside the per-line try, and its own catch ('malformed event — skip') swallows the throw, so the error banner never appears. The optimistic onUpdate({status:'running'}) is never corrected until a reload, so the API's 'error' is shown as 'running'.
  - evidence: Code path: ProjectsHub.tsx:229 sets status 'running'; :253-266 `else if (payload.error) throw new Error(payload.error)` sits inside `try { … } catch { /* malformed event — skip */ }`; the outer catch at :269-272 is never reached. The backend at agentic_core/projects/api.py:388-392 persists 'error' and yields the error frame. Not reproduced live, because the floor does not raise.
  - writers: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/projects/ProjectsHub.tsx:253-266; apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/projects/ProjectsHub.tsx:229
  - fix (the sweeper's proposal — a lead): Parse the JSON inside the try, but handle payload.error outside it: set the error, call onUpdate({...project, status:'error'}), and return. When the stream ends without a done frame, re-fetch the project.
  - skeptic: Confirmed in code at ProjectsHub.tsx:253-266: `else if (payload.error) throw new Error(...)` sits inside `try {...} catch { /* malformed event — skip */ }`, so the outer catch (:269-272) that sets the error and status is never reached. The optimistic 'running' set at :229 persists and no error is shown. Not reproduced live, because the floor does not raise, so it stays at tier 2.

SynthesisStudio.tsx:516 JSON.parses it and renders BusinessModelDashboard. Its :84 LTSA '30% reduction', :88-94 'Adoption Velocity High' with a 3/4 bar, and :106-108 SimParam literals (15% Market Share, 28% Gain, 2.4x) are constants. This is reached only when /synthesis/stream is !ok (SynthesisStudio.tsx:216-222), so tier 2.
